What this evaluates

(agb 0a8c4ee7) Which Claude model should run each planning step of agb (spec, architecture, task breakdown, test design)? Inputs are real agb projects at the moment the step began; every candidate runs agb's own stage command through the same wrapper; a check grades with agb's gates and a judge compares candidates pairwise.

This step (task-breakdown): Task breakdown: turn the PRD, design and API contracts into a task plan (.ab/tasks.json) and a UI contract.

A pass of plan-gates means: The task plan claims every page and endpoint in the design and has no cycle between domains.

directional

Recommend opus-5-5 · highest assurance

$5.2965 per success · required check 3/3 passed

2 eligible candidates tie at 1.000 (3/3) reliability and 1.000 (3/3) required-check pass rate (within the minimum meaningful difference 0.05); opus-5-5 is picked by the judge's pairwise score (opus-5-5 67%, next sonnet-5-5 33%).

Why, in the judge's words: On invoice, against sonnet-5-5: A provides reusable implementation seams before their consumers, notably transitionInvoice in T-021 and the live/fake integration adapters in T-009; B's sending, voiding, and webhook tasks instead describe status changes separately without a shared transition task. Although A still has substantial tasks such as T-024 and T-027, its explicit result handlers, independently scheduled settlement detail, and separated infrastructure work make it the safer plan to hand to an implementing agent.

The judge preferred opus-5-5 most (1 W 0 L 2 T of 3), which matches the recommendation.

Directional only: only 3 input(s) (fewer than 6). Also opus-5-5: required-check-pass-rate 3/3 has a 95% interval down to 0.439, below the gate 1; a gate of 1 can never be firm, declare one such as 0.95; opus-5-5: reliability 3/3 has a 95% interval down to 0.439, below the gate 1; a gate of 1 can never be firm, declare one such as 0.95.

Cost and success one point per candidate · up passes the required check more often, left costs less per success

The cost is the generation cost per success the operating mode ranks on: under "lowest cost" the recommendation is the leftmost filled point above the gate. A point can sit above the gate and still be hollow when another gate (reliability, pass^k) removed it; hover a point for its numbers and the gate that removed it.

Selection each eligibility gate removes candidates, the operating mode picks among the rest · rule select-v4

  1. required-check pass rate ≥ 100% — removed: nobody removed. Remaining: opus-5-5, sonnet-5-5.
  2. reliability ≥ 100% — removed: nobody removed. Remaining: opus-5-5, sonnet-5-5.

Passed every gate: opus-5-5, sonnet-5-5. The judge's win rate is for reference only and takes no part in selection.

Judge scores pairwise win rate · not used by the recommendation

No 1–5 scores: this step's methods does not declare absolute-1-5. That was declared, not missed.

Pairwise win rate (the table above) is a separate question: shown two outputs side by side, which did the judge prefer, 0–100, a tie counting 0.5. It only counts the matches each candidate took part in, so 0 means "lost every match", not "bad output".

```file:UI_CONTRACT.md
# UI Contract

> 锚点由 .ab/prd.json 的页面规格派生，本项目无设计源（Q-002）。
> 页面短名：/ → home，/tasks/new → new，/tasks/:taskId → task-detail，/members → members。
> 控件名是中文，转 kebab 时取其英文义；类型后缀照固定表（按钮 btn、文本输入与文本域 input、下拉 select、开关 toggle、链接 link、卡片 card、弹窗 modal、列表 list）。
> 界面语言：中文，文案逐字照 docs/PRD.md。名字已经是承诺：只能加，不能删改。

## 跨页共用

- `app-toast` — 全局错误提示容器 div（role=alert）；看板卡片推进失败时显示后端返回的原因（IC-06） [when: 有一条错误提示未消失时]

## PAGE-001 任务看板

- `home-current-member-select` — Header 里的「当前成员」select；选项为名册全体成员，选中后该成员的任务卡片加高亮（IC-01）
- `home-new-task-btn` — Header 里的「新建任务」主操作 button；点击导航到 /tasks/new（IC-02）
- `home-members-link` — Header 里的「成员管理」a 链接；点击导航到 /members（IC-07）
- `home-assignee-filter-select` — Toolbar 里的负责人筛选 select；首项「全部负责人」，其后为名册成员；选中后只显示该负责人的卡片（IC-03，AC-11）
- `home-show-done-toggle` — Toolbar 里的「显示已完成」开关 input type=checkbox（role=switch）；开启追加「已完成」列（IC-04，AC-08）
- `home-loading` — 三列骨架占位容器；断言加载态 [when: 任务列表请求未返回时]
- `home-empty` — 空态容器，文案「还没有任务，点击「新建任务」开始」；出现时不渲染任何状态列（AC-09） [when: 任务总数为 0 时]
- `home-error` — 顶部错误条，显示后端返回的原因 [when: 任务列表加载失败时]
- `home-column-todo` — 「待办」列容器 section [when: 任务总数大于 0 时]
- `home-column-in-progress` — 「进行中」列容器 section [when: 任务总数大于 0 时]
- `home-column-done` — 「已完成」列容器 section（AC-07、AC-08） [when: 任务总数大于 0 且「显示已完成」开启时]
- `home-column-count` — 每列标题旁的计数 span，随筛选同步更新（AC-02、IC-03） [when: 任务总数大于 0 时]
- `home-task-card` — 任务卡片 article，带 data-task-id 与 data-status；点击导航到 /tasks/:taskId；当前成员的卡片带 data-highlighted="true"（IC-05、IC-01） [when: 当前可见列里至少有一张卡片时]
- `home-task-card-title` — 卡片上的任务标题文本 [when: 有卡片时]
- `home-task-card-assignee` — 卡片上的负责人姓名文本，未分配时显示「未分配」（AC-11、AC-13） [when: 有卡片时]
- `home-task-card-advance-btn` — 卡片上的状态推进 button：todo 卡片文案「开始处理」，in_progress 卡片文案「标记完成」，done 卡片不渲染（IC-06、Q-007） [when: 卡片状态为 todo 或 in_progress 时]
- `home-workload-list` — 右侧成员工作量列表 ul（RULE-01）
- `home-workload-row` — 工作量列表的一行 li，内容「成员名 · 当前工作量」，带 data-member-id（AC-05、AC-10） [when: 名册至少有一位成员时]
- `home-workload-count` — 行内的工作量数字 span（AC-05、AC-10） [when: 名册至少有一位成员时]

## PAGE-002 新建任务

- `new-title-input` — 标题 input（必填，标签旁有必填标记）；失焦且为空时显示内联校验（IC-08，AC-01）
- `new-title-error` — 标题下方的内联校验文本「标题必填」（AC-01） [when: 标题为空且已失焦时]
- `new-description-input` — 描述 textarea（IC-09）
- `new-assignee-select` — 负责人 select；选项恰为名册成员加一项「未分配」，默认「未分配」（IC-10，AC-04）
- `new-create-btn` — 「创建」button；标题为空时 disabled，提交中 loading 且禁用（IC-11，AC-01、AC-02）
- `new-cancel-btn` — 「取消」button；丢弃草稿并导航回 /（IC-12）
- `new-loading` — 负责人下拉的占位文案；断言名册加载中下拉禁用 [when: 名册请求未返回时]
- `new-error` — 表单上方的提交失败原因，显示后端返回的原因（AC-03） [when: 创建请求失败时]

## PAGE-003 任务详情

- `task-detail-back-link` — 「返回看板」a 链接；导航到 /（IC-17）
- `task-detail-status-badge` — 当前状态徽标 span，文案为「待办」「进行中」「已完成」之一，带 data-status（IC-16）
- `task-detail-title-input` — 标题内联 input；失焦即保存，保存中 disabled（IC-13）
- `task-detail-description-input` — 描述内联 textarea；失焦即保存，保存中 disabled（IC-14）
- `task-detail-assignee-select` — 负责人 select；选项为名册成员加「未分配」，选中即保存（IC-15，AC-05）
- `task-detail-saving` — 被保存字段旁的「保存中」标记 [when: 某字段保存请求未返回时]
- `task-detail-saved` — 被保存字段旁的「已保存」标记 [when: 最近一次字段保存成功后]
- `task-detail-field-error` — 字段保存失败原因文本（字段已回滚为原值） [when: 字段保存失败时]
- `task-detail-transition-start-btn` — 「开始处理」button（AC-06） [when: 状态为 todo 时]
- `task-detail-transition-back-btn` — 「退回待办」button [when: 状态为 in_progress 时]
- `task-detail-transition-done-btn` — 「标记完成」button（AC-07） [when: 状态为 in_progress 时]
- `task-detail-transition-error` — 转移失败原因文本 [when: 转移请求失败时]
- `task-detail-loading` — 字段骨架占位 [when: 任务请求未返回时]
- `task-detail-error` — 「任务不存在」提示容器，内含返回看板链接 [when: taskId 不存在时]

- `members-total` — 成员总数文本
- `members-list` — 成员列表 ul [when: 名册至少有一位成员时]

```file:UI_CONTRACT.md
# UI Contract

> 锚点由 .ab/prd.json 的页面规格派生，本项目无设计源。

## 应用骨架（跨页共用）

- `app-toast-error` — 全局错误 toast 容器；断言操作失败时的原因 [when: 某次写操作失败时]

## PAGE-001 任务看板

- `home-current-member-select` — 当前成员下拉 select；选择会话身份（仅本地）
- `home-new-task-btn` — 新建任务按钮 button；导航到 /tasks/new
- `home-members-link` — 成员管理链接 link；导航到 /members
- `home-assignee-filter-select` — 负责人筛选下拉 select；本地过滤卡片与列计数
- `home-show-done-toggle` — 显示已完成开关 toggle；追加或移除已完成列
- `home-column-todo` — 待办列容器；断言卡片归属
- `home-column-in-progress` — 进行中列容器；断言卡片归属
- `home-column-done` — 已完成列容器；断言 done 卡片 [when: 显示已完成开关开启]
- `home-column-count` — 各列标题旁计数；断言筛选与推进后的数字
- `home-task-card` — 任务卡片 list 项；点击进入详情，断言标题与负责人
- `home-advance-btn` — 卡片状态推进按钮 button；文案为开始处理、退回待办、标记完成，只渲染 WF-01 允许的转移 [when: 任务状态不是 done]
- `home-workload-list` — 侧栏成员工作量列表 list；断言 RULE-01
- `home-workload-row` — 侧栏一行「成员名 · 当前工作量」；数字为负责人为该成员且状态为 todo 或 in_progress 的任务条数
- `home-loading` — 三列骨架占位；加载中 [when: 任务请求进行中]
- `home-empty` — 空态提示「还没有任务，点击「新建任务」开始」 [when: 没有任何任务]
- `home-error` — 顶部错误条含后端原因 [when: 加载失败]
- `home-toast-error` — 推进失败 toast；断言卡片归位后的原因 [when: 状态推进失败]

工作量口径（RULE-01）：负责人为该成员且状态为 `todo` 或 `in_progress` 的任务条数。

## PAGE-002 新建任务

- `new-title-input` — 标题输入框 input；填写标题（必填），例如写周报
- `new-title-error` — 标题下方内联校验「标题必填」 [when: 标题为空并失焦]
- `new-description-input` — 描述文本域 textarea；填写描述
- `new-assignee-select` — 负责人下拉 select；名册加「未分配」，默认未分配
- `new-submit-btn` — 创建按钮 button；标题为空时禁用，提交中 loading
- `new-cancel-btn` — 取消按钮 button；丢弃草稿回到 /
- `new-loading` — 名册加载占位；负责人下拉禁用 [when: 成员名册加载中]
- `new-error` — 表单上方提交失败原因 [when: 创建请求失败]

## PAGE-003 任务详情

- `detail-back-link` — 返回看板链接 link；导航到 /
- `detail-status-badge` — 当前状态徽标；断言状态
- `detail-title-input` — 标题内联编辑 input；失焦保存
- `detail-description-input` — 描述内联编辑 textarea；失焦保存
- `detail-assignee-select` — 负责人下拉 select；立即保存改派
- `detail-transition-btn` — 状态转移按钮组中的一个 button；文案为开始处理、退回待办、标记完成，只渲染 WF-01 允许的转移 [when: 任务状态不是 done]
- `detail-saved-mark` — 已保存标记 [when: 内联保存成功后]
- `detail-save-error` — 保存失败原因 [when: 内联保存或改派失败]
- `detail-transition-error` — 转移失败原因 [when: 状态转移失败]
- `detail-loading` — 字段骨架占位 [when: 任务加载中]
- `detail-error` — 「任务不存在」及返回看板链接 [when: taskId 不存在]

## PAGE-004 成员管理

- `members-back-link` — 返回看板链接 link；导航到 /
- `members-list` — 成员列表 list；断言名册
- `members-row` — 成员列表一行（姓名文本）；断言姓名
- `members-count` — 成员总数；断言重名时条数不变
- `members-name-input` — 新成员姓名输入框 input；回车等价添加
- `members-add-btn` — 添加成员按钮 button；提交中 loading
- `members-duplicate-error` — 输入框下方内联错误「该成员已存在」 [when: 添加重名成员]
- `members-remove-btn` — 每行的移除按钮 button；弹出确认
- `members-remove-confirm` — 移除确认弹窗；确认后成员消失 [when: 点击移除按钮后]
- `members-remove-confirm-btn` — 确认移除按钮 button [when: 确认弹窗打开]
- `members-remove-cancel-btn` — 取消移除按钮 button [when: 确认弹窗打开]
- `members-loading` — 列表骨架占位 [when: 名册加载中]
- `members-empty` — 空态「还没有成员，先添加一位」 [when: 名册为空]
- `members-error` — 读写失败提示含原因 [when: 读写失败]

## 本期不做

- 截止日期、优先级、评论、删除任务、重新打开已完成任务（PRD §2.1 非目标）。
- 登录与鉴权、自动轮询/推送（PRD §9.1、§9.2 默认决策）。

```

Match evidence each dimension judged separately; an order disagreement is recorded as a tie by rule

opus-5-5 vs sonnet-5-5 demo-todo tie

A is the safer implementation handoff because its T-003/T-006 establish explicit shared error handling, T-020 centralizes workflow rules for both client and server, and T-024 specifies forward-only card behavior with concrete verification. B offers better parallel scheduling and board ownership, but its broader bootstrap tasks and looser API and card-action instructions leave more consequential decisions to the implementer.

granularitytie order disagreement: a / bA separates process startup (T-001), test infrastructure (T-002), database/error infrastructure (T-003), and seed tooling (T-004); B bundles startup, schema creation, automatic route discovery, and root scripts into T-001, then combines testing, seeding, and Vite configuration in T-002. A also separates detail editing (T-025) from detail transitions (T-026), whereas B's T-011 implements both alongside reassignment and rollback behavior.
orderingsonnet-5-5 B lets NewTaskPage (T-010), TaskDetailPage (T-011), and BoardPage (T-008) proceed independently once their API and frontend prerequisites exist. A unnecessarily makes creation (T-022) depend on the board (T-021), card transitions (T-024) depend on filtering/sidebar work (T-023), and detail editing (T-025) depend on those card transitions.
clarityopus-5-5 A's T-003 specifies the exact error envelope and status/code behavior, and T-006 defines a shared ApiError client; B's T-004 and T-007 often require only a response containing a reason and even allow alternatives such as 409/400. A's T-024 and UI_CONTRACT.md consistently require one forward-only card action, while B's T-008 says to render WF-01-allowed actions and its home-advance-btn contract includes '退回待办', leaving card behavior less constrained.
domainssonnet-5-5 B gives board ownership its own domain, with T-008/T-009 owning BoardPage, TaskCard, filtering, and workload presentation; A puts T-021 through T-026, spanning board, creation, detail editing, and transitions, into one task domain. B also establishes route and page discovery in T-001/T-003 so feature domains create their own modules, whereas A's member and task domains repeatedly modify shared server/src/app.ts and client/src/App.tsx.
opus-5-5 vs sonnet-5-5 chessicle tie

A offers more implementable sessions rather than merely more tasks: its T-063–T-069 report decomposition replaces B T-045's whole-report assignment, and its standalone T-060 engine task avoids B T-040's unnecessary dependency on completed import endpoints. Its shared legality and HTTP-client tasks, T-008 and T-009, also give implementers explicit reuse boundaries that B's corresponding game and prep tasks do not establish.

granularitytie order disagreement: a / bB T-045 combines the entire report frontend—streaming, summary, move explanations, recommendations, partial-report retry, board and review integration—into one task; A separates these into T-063 through T-069 with individually testable outcomes. Similarly, B T-021 bundles refusal, signed-link revocation and scheduled deletion, while A T-032, T-033 and T-034 make those separate sessions around a shared deletion function.
orderingtie order disagreement: a / bB T-040 makes the Stockfish worker pool depend on T-033, unnecessarily waiting for OCR reconstruction, confirmation and game-list endpoints; A T-060 depends only on platform configuration T-002, allowing engine work in parallel with import. B T-031 also requires progression through reconstructing to awaiting_confirmation before T-032 implements reconstruction, whereas A T-052 consumes the pipeline already implemented by T-051.
clarityopus-5-5 A T-051 specifies concrete upload outcomes including concurrent idempotency, 409 conflicts, 413 size rejection, 415 non-image rejection and terminal-state photo deletion; B T-030 leaves concurrency and size limits unspecified and only declares a lifecycle configuration file. A T-070 explicitly identifies the dismissal endpoint, migration and report-view trigger, while B T-060/T-061 instead direct an implementer toward local-storage dismissal tracking, illustrating materially different and less contract-specific implementation instructions.
domainstie order disagreement: a / bA places shared chess legality in platform T-008 and explicitly requires both OCR reconstruction and frontend confirmation to consume it; B puts chess.js reconstruction in game T-032 but leaves frontend legality validation in T-036 without a shared implementation boundary. A also assigns the common external-call client to platform T-009 for OCR, LLM, search and opponent sources, whereas B places fetch-policy.ts under prep T-050 and provides no equivalent shared boundary for its OCR and LLM clients.
opus-5-5 vs sonnet-5-5 invoice opus-5-5

A provides reusable implementation seams before their consumers, notably transitionInvoice in T-021 and the live/fake integration adapters in T-009; B's sending, voiding, and webhook tasks instead describe status changes separately without a shared transition task. Although A still has substantial tasks such as T-024 and T-027, its explicit result handlers, independently scheduled settlement detail, and separated infrastructure work make it the safer plan to hand to an implementing agent.

granularitytie order disagreement: a / bA separates migrations (T-002), development keys (T-011), and repeatable demo fixtures (T-012), whereas B T-002 bundles database configuration, migrations, comprehensive seeds, demo credentials, and report.login into one task. A also separates styling (T-005) from the typed API client/query cache (T-007), while B T-009 combines those unrelated implementation units.
orderingopus-5-5 A T-041 implements settlement detail after the list task T-038; B T-063 unnecessarily waits for submission T-062, serializing a read-only page behind the entire request flow. A T-048 depends on T-046, which creates the webhook infrastructure it reuses, whereas B T-092 modifies webhook/routes.ts without depending on T-090, the task that creates that file.
clarityopus-5-5 A T-032 names applyVoidResult, specifies Pending/Overdue eligibility, and defines rollback and retained-reason behavior; B T-046 mentions a cancellation callback without naming or assigning its handler and describes rollback only to Pending. A T-045 specifies exact payment return parameters and banner text, while B T-080 asks for a failure or timeout state with retry/contact behavior without defining how an external hosted-payment failure reaches the page.
domainsopus-5-5 A places applyPayoutResult in settlement T-042 and makes webhook T-047 dispatch to that domain-owned function; B puts settlement and invoice payout-result mutations directly in webhook T-091. A also supplies the shared Cobo adapter through platform T-009, whereas B org T-020 owns lib/coboClient.ts even though invoice sending T-042 and settlement submission subsequently depend on that integration.

```file:UI_CONTRACT.md
# UI Contract

> 锚点由 .ab/prd.json 的页面规格派生，本项目无设计源（Q-002）。
> 页面短名：/ → home，/tasks/new → new，/tasks/:taskId → task-detail，/members → members。
> 控件名是中文，转 kebab 时取其英文义；类型后缀照固定表（按钮 btn、文本输入与文本域 input、下拉 select、开关 toggle、链接 link、卡片 card、弹窗 modal、列表 list）。
> 界面语言：中文，文案逐字照 docs/PRD.md。名字已经是承诺：只能加，不能删改。

## 跨页共用

- `app-toast` — 全局错误提示容器 div（role=alert）；看板卡片推进失败时显示后端返回的原因（IC-06） [when: 有一条错误提示未消失时]

## PAGE-001 任务看板

- `home-current-member-select` — Header 里的「当前成员」select；选项为名册全体成员，选中后该成员的任务卡片加高亮（IC-01）
- `home-new-task-btn` — Header 里的「新建任务」主操作 button；点击导航到 /tasks/new（IC-02）
- `home-members-link` — Header 里的「成员管理」a 链接；点击导航到 /members（IC-07）
- `home-assignee-filter-select` — Toolbar 里的负责人筛选 select；首项「全部负责人」，其后为名册成员；选中后只显示该负责人的卡片（IC-03，AC-11）
- `home-show-done-toggle` — Toolbar 里的「显示已完成」开关 input type=checkbox（role=switch）；开启追加「已完成」列（IC-04，AC-08）
- `home-loading` — 三列骨架占位容器；断言加载态 [when: 任务列表请求未返回时]
- `home-empty` — 空态容器，文案「还没有任务，点击「新建任务」开始」；出现时不渲染任何状态列（AC-09） [when: 任务总数为 0 时]
- `home-error` — 顶部错误条，显示后端返回的原因 [when: 任务列表加载失败时]
- `home-column-todo` — 「待办」列容器 section [when: 任务总数大于 0 时]
- `home-column-in-progress` — 「进行中」列容器 section [when: 任务总数大于 0 时]
- `home-column-done` — 「已完成」列容器 section（AC-07、AC-08） [when: 任务总数大于 0 且「显示已完成」开启时]
- `home-column-count` — 每列标题旁的计数 span，随筛选同步更新（AC-02、IC-03） [when: 任务总数大于 0 时]
- `home-task-card` — 任务卡片 article，带 data-task-id 与 data-status；点击导航到 /tasks/:taskId；当前成员的卡片带 data-highlighted="true"（IC-05、IC-01） [when: 当前可见列里至少有一张卡片时]
- `home-task-card-title` — 卡片上的任务标题文本 [when: 有卡片时]
- `home-task-card-assignee` — 卡片上的负责人姓名文本，未分配时显示「未分配」（AC-11、AC-13） [when: 有卡片时]
- `home-task-card-advance-btn` — 卡片上的状态推进 button：todo 卡片文案「开始处理」，in_progress 卡片文案「标记完成」，done 卡片不渲染（IC-06、Q-007） [when: 卡片状态为 todo 或 in_progress 时]
- `home-workload-list` — 右侧成员工作量列表 ul（RULE-01）
- `home-workload-row` — 工作量列表的一行 li，内容「成员名 · 当前工作量」，带 data-member-id（AC-05、AC-10） [when: 名册至少有一位成员时]
- `home-workload-count` — 行内的工作量数字 span（AC-05、AC-10） [when: 名册至少有一位成员时]

## PAGE-002 新建任务

- `new-title-input` — 标题 input（必填，标签旁有必填标记）；失焦且为空时显示内联校验（IC-08，AC-01）
- `new-title-error` — 标题下方的内联校验文本「标题必填」（AC-01） [when: 标题为空且已失焦时]
- `new-description-input` — 描述 textarea（IC-09）
- `new-assignee-select` — 负责人 select；选项恰为名册成员加一项「未分配」，默认「未分配」（IC-10，AC-04）
- `new-create-btn` — 「创建」button；标题为空时 disabled，提交中 loading 且禁用（IC-11，AC-01、AC-02）
- `new-cancel-btn` — 「取消」button；丢弃草稿并导航回 /（IC-12）
- `new-loading` — 负责人下拉的占位文案；断言名册加载中下拉禁用 [when: 名册请求未返回时]
- `new-error` — 表单上方的提交失败原因，显示后端返回的原因（AC-03） [when: 创建请求失败时]

## PAGE-003 任务详情

- `task-detail-back-link` — 「返回看板」a 链接；导航到 /（IC-17）
- `task-detail-status-badge` — 当前状态徽标 span，文案为「待办」「进行中」「已完成」之一，带 data-status（IC-16）
- `task-detail-title-input` — 标题内联 input；失焦即保存，保存中 disabled（IC-13）
- `task-detail-description-input` — 描述内联 textarea；失焦即保存，保存中 disabled（IC-14）
- `task-detail-assignee-select` — 负责人 select；选项为名册成员加「未分配」，选中即保存（IC-15，AC-05）
- `task-detail-saving` — 被保存字段旁的「保存中」标记 [when: 某字段保存请求未返回时]
- `task-detail-saved` — 被保存字段旁的「已保存」标记 [when: 最近一次字段保存成功后]
- `task-detail-field-error` — 字段保存失败原因文本（字段已回滚为原值） [when: 字段保存失败时]
- `task-detail-transition-start-btn` — 「开始处理」button（AC-06） [when: 状态为 todo 时]
- `task-detail-transition-back-btn` — 「退回待办」button [when: 状态为 in_progress 时]
- `task-detail-transition-done-btn` — 「标记完成」button（AC-07） [when: 状态为 in_progress 时]
- `task-detail-transition-error` — 转移失败原因文本 [when: 转移请求失败时]
- `task-detail-loading` — 字段骨架占位 [when: 任务请求未返回时]
- `task-detail-error` — 「任务不存在」提示容器，内含返回看板链接 [when: taskId 不存在时]

## PAGE-004 成员管理

- `members-back-link` — 「返回看板」a 链接；导航到 /（IC-21）
- `members-total` — 成员总数文本
- `members-list` — 成员列表 ul [when: 名册至少有一位成员时]
- `members-
sonnet-5-5 · demo-todo · t0 19,330 characters

```file:UI_CONTRACT.md
# UI Contract

> 锚点由 .ab/prd.json 的页面规格派生，本项目无设计源。

## 应用骨架（跨页共用）

- `app-toast-error` — 全局错误 toast 容器；断言操作失败时的原因 [when: 某次写操作失败时]

## PAGE-001 任务看板

- `home-current-member-select` — 当前成员下拉 select；选择会话身份（仅本地）
- `home-new-task-btn` — 新建任务按钮 button；导航到 /tasks/new
- `home-members-link` — 成员管理链接 link；导航到 /members
- `home-assignee-filter-select` — 负责人筛选下拉 select；本地过滤卡片与列计数
- `home-show-done-toggle` — 显示已完成开关 toggle；追加或移除已完成列
- `home-column-todo` — 待办列容器；断言卡片归属
- `home-column-in-progress` — 进行中列容器；断言卡片归属
- `home-column-done` — 已完成列容器；断言 done 卡片 [when: 显示已完成开关开启]
- `home-column-count` — 各列标题旁计数；断言筛选与推进后的数字
- `home-task-card` — 任务卡片 list 项；点击进入详情，断言标题与负责人
- `home-advance-btn` — 卡片状态推进按钮 button；文案为开始处理、退回待办、标记完成，只渲染 WF-01 允许的转移 [when: 任务状态不是 done]
- `home-workload-list` — 侧栏成员工作量列表 list；断言 RULE-01
- `home-workload-row` — 侧栏一行「成员名 · 当前工作量」；数字为负责人为该成员且状态为 todo 或 in_progress 的任务条数
- `home-loading` — 三列骨架占位；加载中 [when: 任务请求进行中]
- `home-empty` — 空态提示「还没有任务，点击「新建任务」开始」 [when: 没有任何任务]
- `home-error` — 顶部错误条含后端原因 [when: 加载失败]
- `home-toast-error` — 推进失败 toast；断言卡片归位后的原因 [when: 状态推进失败]

工作量口径（RULE-01）：负责人为该成员且状态为 `todo` 或 `in_progress` 的任务条数。

## PAGE-002 新建任务

- `new-title-input` — 标题输入框 input；填写标题（必填），例如写周报
- `new-title-error` — 标题下方内联校验「标题必填」 [when: 标题为空并失焦]
- `new-description-input` — 描述文本域 textarea；填写描述
- `new-assignee-select` — 负责人下拉 select；名册加「未分配」，默认未分配
- `new-submit-btn` — 创建按钮 button；标题为空时禁用，提交中 loading
- `new-cancel-btn` — 取消按钮 button；丢弃草稿回到 /
- `new-loading` — 名册加载占位；负责人下拉禁用 [when: 成员名册加载中]
- `new-error` — 表单上方提交失败原因 [when: 创建请求失败]

## PAGE-003 任务详情

- `detail-back-link` — 返回看板链接 link；导航到 /
- `detail-status-badge` — 当前状态徽标；断言状态
- `detail-title-input` — 标题内联编辑 input；失焦保存
- `detail-description-input` — 描述内联编辑 textarea；失焦保存
- `detail-assignee-select` — 负责人下拉 select；立即保存改派
- `detail-transition-btn` — 状态转移按钮组中的一个 button；文案为开始处理、退回待办、标记完成，只渲染 WF-01 允许的转移 [when: 任务状态不是 done]
- `detail-saved-mark` — 已保存标记 [when: 内联保存成功后]
- `detail-save-error` — 保存失败原因 [when: 内联保存或改派失败]
- `detail-transition-error` — 转移失败原因 [when: 状态转移失败]
- `detail-loading` — 字段骨架占位 [when: 任务加载中]
- `detail-error` — 「任务不存在」及返回看板链接 [when: taskId 不存在]

## PAGE-004 成员管理

- `members-back-link` — 返回看板链接 link；导航到 /
- `members-list` — 成员列表 list；断言名册
- `members-row` — 成员列表一行（姓名文本）；断言姓名
- `members-count` — 成员总数；断言重名时条数不变
- `members-name-input` — 新成员姓名输入框 input；回车等价添加
- `members-add-btn` — 添加成员按钮 button；提交中 loading
- `members-duplicate-error` — 输入框下方内联错误「该成员已存在」 [when: 添加重名成员]
- `members-remove-btn` — 每行的移除按钮 button；弹出确认
- `members-remove-confirm` — 移除确认弹窗；确认后成员消失 [when: 点击移除按钮后]
- `members-remove-confirm-btn` — 确认移除按钮 button [when: 确认弹窗打开]
- `members-remove-cancel-btn` — 取消移除按钮 button [when: 确认弹窗打开]
- `members-loading` — 列表骨架占位 [when: 名册加载中]
- `members-empty` — 空态「还没有成员，先添加一位」 [when: 名册为空]
- `members-error` — 读写失败提示含原因 [when: 读写失败]

## 本期不做

- 截止日期、优先级、评论、删除任务、重新打开已完成任务（PRD §2.1 非目标）。
- 登录与鉴权、自动轮询/推送（PRD §9.1、§9.2 默认决策）。

Fields this run did not observe GAPS.md

  • ttft_ms: non-streaming calls; no time to first token.
  • cache: the provider reported cached prompt tokens on some calls; recorded under tokens.cached, no cache cost breakdown.
  • deployment_ref.region / tier: who served the model is read from the command's trace (cli/…), from OpenRouter's upstream provider, or from the model id a direct API answered with; region and service tier are not exposed.
  • human_intervention: not observed; no trace format read here says whether the agent waited on a person.
  • tool_calls, turns: observed for every trial from the trace the command left; counts in agent per row, the turn list in traces/.
  • checkpoint: not observed; no trace format read here says whether the agent resumed from one.
  • transport retry: model-API calls retry transient failures (HTTP 408/409/425/429, 5xx, network) twice with exponential backoff and no jitter; per-trial retry counts are not in scores.jsonl, only retry cost.
  • evaluator errors: none in this run.
  • absolute 1–5 scoring: **not run** — the spec declares methods without absolute-1-5. Magnitude is therefore not observed at all in this run; the empty score columns are a declaration, not a failure.
  • effective N: 0 of 3 inputs separated any two candidates on task success; 3 every candidate passed, 0 gave everyone the same partial rate. Only the separating inputs carry evidence for a choice (items.json). The judge preferred one candidate over another on 1 of 3.
  • pairwise judging uses only the first successful output per (candidate, input); repeated trials feed absolute scores only. The selector reads the pairwise score only to rank judge-preference, and to break a tie on assurance in highest-assurance.
  • statistical uncertainty: the chosen candidate's gated rates carry Wilson 95% intervals, and a highest-assurance pick must beat the runner-up on an exact sign test over inputs (recommendation.json evidence). Inputs generated from one template are still treated as independent, which overstates certainty when they share a template.
  • **combined run**: built from agb-flow-v3-2026-11-13T10-23-03-124Z (later runs supersede earlier ones per trial); nothing was generated or judged for it. combined-from.json says which run each trial came from.
